# KADENCE Music Refresh Report

Date: 21 November 2026 · Snapshot: `docs/music-refresh/charts-2026-11-21.json` (generated 2026-11-21T10:32:40.736Z)

## Summary

Charts were outdated because the selection was **keyword search, not chart data**. Only the first three hardcoded search terms per chart ever ran (some naming 2023/2024), refresh returned the same cached response, and the regional "playlist" data files were empty. KADENCE now builds every chart from **Apple's live top-songs charts** (official iTunes RSS per storefront and genre, updated daily, no key), adds **50 verified tracks from this season's Tamil soundtracks**, and uses keyword search only to top a chart up. Refresh now returns a genuinely different selection under its own cache key.

**Curated counts:** each song is counted once, under "From Apple charts" if it is also on Apple's live chart. **Top Tamil:** all 50 verified soundtrack tracks are present (29 counted as "Curated", 21 also chart). **Top Hindi:** all 14 unique *Ramayana: Part 1* recordings (17 releases) are present (9 counted as "Curated", 5 also chart). Top Hindi was re-validated live after the Ramayana additions.

"Kept / Added / Removed" compare against the songs the **previous pipeline** served this morning (snapshot taken at 2026-11-21T08:21:40.178Z while building the artwork archive, `artwork-library/.collected.json`). Previous chart sizes: Global Top 50 256, Viral 50 256, New Music Friday 256, Hip-Hop Central 256, Pop Rising 256, Dance Hits 256, Mood Booster 256, Late Night 256, Workout 256, Chill Hits 256, Top Telugu 90, Top Tamil 152, Top Hindi 256, Top K-Pop 65, Dev's Universe (unchanged) 79.

## 1. Root cause of outdated music

1. **Only three search terms ever ran.** `fetchSongsByVibe(vibe, target, refreshOffset)` rotated its query list by `refreshOffset`, but `/api/songs` never passed it, so every load used queries 1–3 of each chart. The other 5–9 terms were dead.
2. **Hardcoded, ageing search terms.** "top hits 2024", "hip hop 2024", "indie pop 2024", "Tollywood soundtrack 2023"… The iTunes Search API ranks by keyword relevance, not recency or popularity, so these returned the same back catalogue indefinitely.
3. **Refresh returned the same response.** `/api/songs?refresh=1` was answered from the 20-minute server cache (only the response header changed), and the client's refresh could be absorbed by an in-flight normal request.
4. **Layered caches stretched staleness.** Server memory 20 min + CDN `s-maxage=1200, stale-while-revalidate=3600` (up to ~100 min) + client 30 min + iTunes client 30 min; and a rate-limited, half-empty result was cached for the full 20 minutes like a good one.
5. **Regional "playlist" data was empty.** `lib/tamilChartData.ts` / `teluguChartData.ts` / `hindiChartData.ts` are generated by scraping Spotify web pages (`scripts/build-regional-charts.mjs`); that scrape no longer yields tracks, so the files are empty arrays and the regional charts silently fell back to the same keyword search. Had they been populated, they would have overridden live data forever.
6. **No popularity signal.** Order was "newest release first among keyword hits", and `popularity` was a hash of the track id.

## 2. What changed

| Area | Change |
|---|---|
| `lib/itunesClient.ts` | New `topSongs(storefront, genre)` reads Apple's documented iTunes top-songs RSS (JSON). Each entry already includes track id, title, artist, album, artwork, genre, release date and the official 30 s preview. Shares the existing cache, single-flight, 3-request concurrency and 20-request/minute budget; charts cached 2 h, searches 30 min. |
| `lib/musicProvider.ts` | Charts are built from Apple charts (merged across storefronts: a song's score is the sum over charts of `1 − (rank−1)/length`), then verified curated tracks, then at most **2** keyword queries only if the chart is short. Order: fresh chart hits and curated new releases → established chart hits → top-up (newest first). Search terms use the current year at run time. Static-array precedence removed. |
| Quality gates | Every song must have a preview **and** artwork. Regional/K-Pop charts accept only Apple's matching genre (Tamil / Telugu / Bollywood·Hindi / K-Pop) and still reject wrong-script and karaoke/BGM compilations. Derivative uploads (karaoke, ringtone, "made famous", sped-up/slowed, 8-bit…) are rejected everywhere. One entry per song across single/album/"(From …)" re-releases. Artist and album caps for diversity; whole verified soundtracks are spread across the wall instead of clustered. New Music Friday keeps releases ≤ 90 days, Viral 50 ≤ 150 days. |
| `popularity` | Now derived from Apple chart position (55–100) where a song charts; curated non-charting 60; top-up 30–45 by recency. Shown as KADENCE's "discovery score" — not an external popularity figure. |
| `SongNode` | Optional `releaseDate`, `source` (`chart`/`curated`/`search`), `chartRank`. Existing fields unchanged; the 3D wall and atlas are untouched. |
| `lib/curatedTracks.ts` + `scripts/build-curated-tracks.mjs` | Verified Tamil soundtracks, fetched from the iTunes Lookup API by album/track id and rejected unless Tamil genre + preview + artwork. Re-run the script to refresh. |

## 3. Cache and refresh fixes

- **Rotation:** each refresh increments a per-chart counter on the client (`lib/vibeService.ts`) sent as `/api/songs?vibe=…&rotation=n`. Rotation selects the next keyword batch (all query terms are reached over successive refreshes) and brings a different stretch of the chart to the centre of the wall.
- **Cache keys:** server cache and in-flight de-duplication are keyed by `chart:rotation` (12 rotations, then wrap); the CDN caches each rotation URL separately, so a refresh never receives the previous response and Apple is asked at most once per chart/rotation per window.
- **Partial results:** if any Apple chart could not be read (rate limit, timeout) the chart is cached 2 min instead of 20, with a short CDN lifetime, so a degraded chart heals quickly.
- **CDN freshness:** `stale-while-revalidate` lowered from 3600 s to 1800 s; 24 h stale fallback kept only for outright upstream failure.
- **Request budget:** a cold chart costs 1–5 chart feeds (shared and cached 2 h across charts) + 0–2 searches, inside the client's 3-concurrent / 20-per-minute guard.

## 4. Top Tamil — verified soundtracks

All four soundtracks were confirmed as Tamil releases from Apple's metadata (Apple genre **Tamil**, India storefront) and cross-checked against press coverage:

- **Jailer 2** — Anirudh Ravichander. Full 12-track Tamil album (Apple collection 6817733404) plus singles *Roddy Theme*, *Tiger Muthuvel Pandian* and *Unfinished Business* (released 21 Nov 2026). Apple also lists Telugu (6822181956), Kannada (6823038209), "Regional Indian" (6826093176) and Hindi "Rajini The Jailer 2" (6822182859) dubbed albums — excluded from Top Tamil.
- **Scene** — Sushin Shyam; Suriya, dir. Jithu Madhavan, released 6 Nov 2026. Full 17-track Tamil album (6831048832). The separate "Scene (Telugu)" album is excluded.
- **Baththa** — Sai Abhyankkar; Vijay Sethupathi, dir. Balaji Tharaneetharan, released 1 Oct 2026. Full 10-track album (6818555484), including *Jiguchikkan* (track 4 on Apple's official tracklist; not mentioned in the press results found).
- **O.M: Operation Mole** — Sai Abhyankkar; Dhanush, dir. Rajkumar Periasamy, released 20 Nov 2026. **Attribution note:** this is the same film earlier titled *OM Chapter 1: Udhiram – The Blood Wood* (renamed a week before release). Apple credits earlier singles to the old title and *Senjudare* and *FA FA FA* to "O.M: Operation Mole"; both belong to this one film. Telugu (*Veera Veera*, *Alaakaa Loova* [Telugu]) and Hindi ("O.M: Operation Mole - Hindi") versions are excluded.

## 4b. Top Hindi — Ramayana: Part 1 (2026), every track including score/BGM

*Ramayana: Part 1* (dir. Nitesh Tiwari; Ranbir Kapoor, Sai Pallavi; music Hans Zimmer & A.R. Rahman, lyrics Kumar Vishwas; in cinemas 5 Nov 2026, Part 2 due Diwali 2027). **Every official Hindi release** is curated into Top Hindi — the 5-track soundtrack EP (Apple collection 6825357149) and every theme and song single. All are ℗ 2026 Super Cassettes Industries (the film's label) in Apple's Bollywood genre. The EP's tracklist matches the published one exactly (Jai Jai Ram, Ravana Tandav, Sita's Aspiration, Jai Jai Ram (Orchestral), Rama Theme).

The 17 releases below are **14 unique recordings**: *Jai Jai Ram*, *Ravana Tandav* and *Rama Theme* were released both as singles and on the EP, and appear once each on the wall. Live validation placed all 14 within the first 29 positions of Top Hindi; five are on Apple's live Bollywood chart (Jai Jai Ram #2, Mangal Rang #5, Shree Hanuman Chalisa #6, Judi Jodi #10, The Introduction Theme #18).

**Excluded:** the Tamil, Telugu, Kannada and Malayalam versions (other languages — the Tamil ones appear in Top Tamil via Apple's Tamil chart); fan-made releases using the name — *Vanvaas (From "Ramayana")* by A.P & Aarohi Noor (℗ Sounds by A.P), *Vanvaas | Namiit Malhotraa's Ramayana* by Shobit singh, *Ramayana Unreleased Soundtrack* by Snehasis Rath, *Rama – Warrior Theme* by Styzmask, and devotional/other albums titled "Ramayana".

## 5. Rejected or unavailable tracks

**Considered for the curated Tamil list and left out:**

| Track | Reason |
|---|---|
| Dubbed albums of Jailer 2 (Telugu, Kannada, Regional Indian, Hindi "Rajini The Jailer 2"), Scene (Telugu), OM/O.M (Telugu, Hindi) | Not Tamil-language releases (Apple genre Telugu / Kannada / Regional Indian / Bollywood / Soundtrack) |
| *Best Of Dhanush* compilation (6836829752) copies of Veezha Veera, Alaakaa Loova, FA FA FA, The Wild Theme | Duplicates of the original singles |
| *The Blood Wood Theme* (Ashwin Azer, "Extra track", genre Worldwide) | Not credited to the film's composer; attribution uncertain |
| *Bindaas x Hukum Theme* mashup single | Derivative mashup of tracks already included |
| Fan-made "Jailer 2" singles (other artists; genres Alternative, Electronic, Pop, Hip-Hop, Dance, piano covers, BGM edits) | Not part of the official soundtrack |


**Filtered automatically at run time (every chart):** songs without a 30 s preview or artwork; regional/K-Pop songs whose Apple genre is not the chart's language; wrong-script or karaoke/BGM compilations in regional charts; derivative uploads (karaoke, ringtone, "made famous", tribute, sped-up/slowed, 8-bit); duplicate releases of the same song; over-represented artists/albums; New Music Friday > 90 days and Viral 50 > 150 days old.

## 6. Validation results

**Live data validation** — `node --import ./scripts/test-loader.mjs scripts/validate-charts.mjs` (production `fetchChart`, live Apple data, 21 Nov 2026): **all 15 charts pass, exit 0.** Top Hindi was re-validated live after the Ramayana additions (also passed).

Checks per chart: no duplicate track ids; no duplicate songs (versions and re-releases collapsed); every song has a preview and artwork; **every preview URL and every artwork URL responded** (HEAD or 1-byte range request, with retries); regional and K-Pop charts contain only their language genre; every curated Tamil track is present. All 15 charts were built `complete` (no Apple chart failed). Snapshot: `docs/music-refresh/charts-2026-11-21.json`.

Two earlier validation runs found real defects, which were fixed before the passing run:
1. Duplicate versions of one recording (e.g. *Levels* / *Levels (Radio Edit)*, *BAD TIMES* / *BAD TIMES (Long Version)*, remasters). Song matching now ignores bracketed version text.
2. Curated Tamil songs that also chart were cut by the per-album/per-artist cap. The curated flag now survives merging.

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| TypeScript (`tsc --noEmit`) | Pass |
| Unit tests (`npm test`) | 45 / 45 pass. New tests cover Ramayana coverage in Top Hindi (all 14 recordings, each once), chart parsing, chart-first ordering, current-year search terms, the preview/artwork gate, the language gate, version de-duplication, the new-music age window, the 2-year top-up limit, refresh rotation, partial-result marking, curated songs that chart, and Dev's Universe |
| ESLint (changed files) | 0 errors, 0 warnings |
| Production build (`next build`) | Pass: compiled, TypeScript passed, all 6 routes generated. Built from an isolated copy so the `next start` server on port 3100 was not affected |

## 7. Remaining limitations

- **No "viral" or "new music" chart exists in Apple's public feeds.** Viral 50 = songs on current Apple charts released in the last 150 days; New Music Friday = alternative/singer-songwriter/pop chart songs from the last 90 days plus newest-first search top-up. Mood Booster, Workout, Chill Hits and Late Night use the closest genre charts plus mood keywords — there is no mood chart.
- **Apple's charts are per country**; "Global" merges US, UK, Canada, Australia and India. There is no worldwide feed, and South Korea's storefront returns no chart, so K-Pop uses the K-Pop genre chart of four English-language storefronts.
- **Short genre charts are topped up by keyword search** (Hip-Hop charts list only 21–40 songs per storefront). Top-up songs are labelled `source: "search"`, sit after chart songs, and are ordered by release date, not treated as popular.
- **Some genre charts are evergreen-heavy by nature.** Apple's US R&B/Soul and Rock charts keep classics (Motown, 70s–90s rock) in their top 100 because people still buy them, so Late Night and Workout have high median release ages even though their leading songs are 2026 releases. KADENCE keeps these as "still-relevant catalogue" (they are on today's chart) and orders fresh releases first.
- **Dev's Universe** was not modified; one of its 79 static songs has no preview URL in the existing data.
- **Song lists change daily with Apple's charts.** The added/removed lists below are today's snapshot; tomorrow's chart can differ — that is the intended freshness.
- **Curated tracks are a fixed, verified list.** Re-run `node scripts/build-curated-tracks.mjs` to re-verify, or edit its SOURCES to add releases.
- **Signature songs** (first track played on chart selection, `lib/signatureSongs.ts`) were left unchanged, as instructed; several are 2019–2024 hits.
- The static Spotify-scrape files (`lib/*ChartData.ts`, `scripts/build-regional-charts.mjs`, `lib/chartSource.ts`) are no longer consulted for song selection; they are left in place because `scripts/audit-media.mjs` imports `chartSource`.
- Not verified in a browser (per instructions).
